  [1m■ OP HISTORY 4: the ops history says come next (on AF205)[0m
  Learned from this chart's past: after an op, how often the next op was the same, sign flipped, ±1/±2, cut switched, or crossed xy/yx. A applies it on the EP, B on the ROOT. Walk-forward
